```text
import path from "path";

const SECRET_PATTERNS: RegExp[] = [
  /(authorization\s*:\s*(?:bearer|basic)\s+)[^\s]+/gi,
  /\b([A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*(?:KEY|TOKEN|SECRET|PASSWORD|PASSWD|COOKIE|CREDENTIALS?)\s*=\s*)[^\s]+/gi,
  /\b(DATABASE_URL|REDIS_URL|MONGODB_URI|AMQP_URL)\s*=\s*[^\s]+/gi,
  /\b((?:cookie|set-cookie|x-api-key|api-key)\s*:\s*)[^\r\n]+/gi,
  /\b(sk-(?:proj|ant|live|test|or)-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,})\b/g,
  /\b(gh[opusr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{20,})\b/g,
  /-----BEGIN [A-Z ]*PRIVATE KEY-----[\s\S]*?-----END [A-Z ]*PRIVATE KEY-----/g,
];

const MACHINE_PATH_PATTERNS: RegExp[] = [
  /(?:\/Users|\/home|\/root|\/private\/var|\/var\/folders)\/[A-Za-z0-9._@+~-]+(?:\/[A-Za-z0-9._@+~ -]+)+/g,
  /\b[A-Za-z]:\\Users\\[^\s"'`]+/g,
];

export function redactHandoffText(value: string | null | undefined, maxCharacters = 4_000): string {
  let result = String(value ?? "").normalize("NFC").replace(/[\u0000-\u0008\u000B\u000C\u000E-\u001F\u007F]/g, "");
  for (const pattern of SECRET_PATTERNS) {
    result = result.replace(pattern, (_match, prefix?: string) => `${prefix ?? ""}[REDACTED]`);
  }
  for (const pattern of MACHINE_PATH_PATTERNS) result = result.replace(pattern, "[REDACTED_PATH]");
  return result.slice(0, Math.max(0, maxCharacters));
}

export function sanitizeProjectRelativePath(value: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  const candidate = String(value ?? "").trim().replaceAll("\\", "/");
  if (!candidate || path.posix.isAbsolute(candidate) || /^[A-Za-z]:\//.test(candidate)) return null;
  const normalized = path.posix.normalize(candidate.replace(/^\.\//, ""));
  if (!normalized || normalized === "." || normalized === ".." || normalized.startsWith("../")) return null;
  return normalized;
}

export function redactHandoffList(values: readonly string[] | null | undefined, maxItems = 40, maxCharacters = 1_000): string[] {
  return [...new Set((values ?? []).map((value) => redactHandoffText(value, maxCharacters)).filter(Boolean))]
    .sort((left, right) => left.localeCompare(right))
    .slice(0, maxItems);
}
```

## Claude thinking · 2026-09-09T09:33:14.812Z

I'm noticing `getAppRoot` in migration.ts and comparing it to the `resolveProfilesDir` logic in external-credentials, which falls back to the current working directory — it makes sense to reuse the shared resolver instead. So I'll export `resolveProfilesDir` as `resolveCredentialProfilesDir` and tighten up its error message.

Then I need to add redaction logic for secrets in provider stderr output, matching key/token patterns and common API key formats before truncating the message to a fixed length.

Adding value patterns for API key formats like sk-, gh_ tokens, and JWT-style strings. Now I'm checking whether an error message like "invalid_refresh_token" in stderr would accidentally get flagged, since the first pattern needs an uppercase key name followed by `=` or `:` — this lowercase snake_case string shouldn't match.

But I realize a JSON body like `{"refresh_token": "..."}` from a provider could have a lowercase key that wouldn't get redacted, so I'm considering adding a case-insensitive variant targeting keys ending in key/token/secret/password/credentials, but checking whether it would false-positive on `"error":"invalid_refresh_token"` since the key there is `error`, not one ending in those terms.

I decide to keep the redaction scoped to uppercase env-style assignments, authorization headers, and known token shapes, since a broader case-insensitive match risks eating parts of the real diagnostic message. Now I'm defining a function to build the provider failure detail string...

I'm wrapping this into a runtime HTTP error that reports the failed provider command with its exit code and detail message. The nested parentheses in the format look a bit ugly, so I'm considering a cleaner separator, but I need to check whether any tests depend on the exact existing message format before changing it.

I settle on putting the status (exit code, signal, timeout, or "command not found") in parentheses, followed by the provider's own error output as a plain sentence. I'm building a helper that summarizes the provider's stderr output and appends it to the base failure message, with special handling for ENOENT to include the expanded command path in the summary. I also note I need to handle cases where output exceeds the max buffer size.

Now I'm looking at the migration code, which needs to resolve the credential profiles directory using environment settings and pull the relevant configuration value from stored settings rows, while preserving the existing behavior of returning an empty list when the directory doesn't exist. I'm pausing to consider whether this change could affect existing behavior elsewhere in the migration logic.

I need to check whether `getAppRoot()`, used elsewhere in migration, aligns with the resolver's fallback of `process.cwd()` when `OMNIHARNESS_ROOT` isn't set — there could be a subtle mismatch that breaks consistency between what the runtime uses and what migration resolves to.

Let me see if getAppRoot offers a more robust approach to this.

```text
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";

function isPathInside(candidate: string, parent: string) {
  const relative = path.relative(parent, candidate);
  return relative !== "" && relative !== ".." && !relative.startsWith(`..${path.sep}`) && !path.isAbsolute(relative);
}

function isTestProcess() {
  return process.env.VITEST === "true"
    || process.env.VITEST_WORKER_ID !== undefined
    || process.env.TEST_WORKER_INDEX !== undefined;
}

function assertSafeTestRoot(root: string) {
  if (!isTestProcess()) {
    return;
  }

  const resolvedRoot = path.resolve(root);
  const resolvedCwd = path.resolve(process.cwd());
  const temporaryParents = [os.tmpdir(), "/tmp", "/var/tmp"].map((entry) => path.resolve(entry));
  const isTemporary = temporaryParents.some((parent) => isPathInside(resolvedRoot, parent));

  if (resolvedRoot === resolvedCwd || !isTemporary) {
    throw new Error(
      `[database-safety] Refusing to use non-temporary app data root in a test process: ${resolvedRoot}`,
    );
  }
}

export function getAppRoot() {
  const configuredRoot = process.env.OMNIHARNESS_ROOT?.trim();
  const root = configuredRoot ? path.resolve(configuredRoot) : process.cwd();
  assertSafeTestRoot(root);
  return root;
}

export function getAppDataPath(...segments: string[]) {
  return path.join(getAppRoot(), ...segments);
}
```

```json
{
```

```json
  "file_path": "/Users/masterman/NLP/omniharness/src/server/agent-runtime/external-credentials.ts",
  "old_string": "function runProviderCommand(command: string, args: string[], input: ResolveCredentialProfileInput, profileDir: string, timeoutMs: number, profileName?: string) {\n  const expanded = expandHome(command, input.env);\n  return new Promise<CredentialProviderResult>((resolveProvider, rejectProvider) => {\n    execFile(expanded, args, {\n      cwd: profileDir,\n      env: input.env as NodeJS.ProcessEnv,\n      timeout: timeoutMs,\n      maxBuffer: MAX_PROVIDER_BUFFER_BYTES,\n    }, (error, stdout) => {\n      if (error) {\n        rejectProvider(new RuntimeHttpError(\n          400,\n          `Credential profile \"${profileName || input.requestedProfile || input.configuredProfile || input.type}\" provider command failed.`,\n        ));\n        return;\n      }",
  "new_string": "function summarizeProviderStderr(stderr: string) {\n  let text = stderr;\n  for (const pattern of PROVIDER_SECRET_ASSIGNMENT_PATTERNS) {\n    text = text.replace(pattern, (_match, prefix: string) => `${prefix}[REDACTED]`);\n  }\n  for (const pattern of PROVIDER_SECRET_VALUE_PATTERNS) {\n    text = text.replace(pattern, \"[REDACTED]\");\n  }\n  return text.replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").trim().slice(0, MAX_PROVIDER_STDERR_CHARS);\n}\n\nfunction describeProviderFailure(error: Error & { code?: string | number; signal?: string; killed?: boolean }, command: string, timeoutMs: number) {\n  if (error.code === \"ENOENT\") return `command not found: ${command}`;\n  if (error.killed) return `timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms`;\n  if (typeof error.code === \"number\") return `exit ${error.code}`;\n  if (typeof error.code === \"string\") return error.code;\n  if (error.signal) return `signal ${error.signal}`;\n  return \"no exit status\";\n}\n\nfunction runProviderCommand(command: string, args: string[], input: ResolveCredentialProfileInput, profileDir: string, timeoutMs: number, profileName?: string) {\n  const expanded = expandHome(command, input.env);\n  return new Promise<CredentialProviderResult>((resolveProvider, rejectProvider) => {\n    execFile(expanded, args, {\n      cwd: profileDir,\n      env: input.env as NodeJS.ProcessEnv,\n      timeout: timeoutMs,\n      maxBuffer: MAX_PROVIDER_BUFFER_BYTES,\n    }, (error, stdout, stderr) => {\n      if (error) {\n        const name = profileName || input.requestedProfile || input.configuredProfile || input.type;\n        const cause = describeProviderFailure(error, expanded, timeoutMs);\n        const detail = summarizeProviderStderr(String(stderr ?? \"\"));\n        rejectProvider(new RuntimeHttpError(\n          400,\n          `Credential profile \"${name}\" provider command failed (${cause}).${detail ? ` ${detail}` : \"\"}`,\n        ));\n        return;\n      }"
}
```

I want to check tests/setup.ts to confirm how testRoot is scoped per worker before finalizing this.

```json
  "content": "import { chmodSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, writeFileSync } from \"fs\";\nimport { tmpdir } from \"os\";\nimport { join } from \"path\";\nimport { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\nimport { resolveCredentialProfile, resolveCredentialProfilesDir } from \"@/server/agent-runtime/external-credentials\";\n\nfunction createProfilesDir() {\n  return mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), \"omni-external-credentials-\"));\n}\n\nfunction writeProviderProfile(profilesDir: string, name: string, script: string) {\n  const profileDir = join(profilesDir, name);\n  mkdirSync(profileDir, { recursive: true });\n  const provider = join(profileDir, \"provider\");\n  writeFileSync(provider, script);\n  chmodSync(provider, 0o755);\n  writeFileSync(join(profileDir, \"profile.json\"), JSON.stringify({ command: provider, timeoutMs: 5_000 }));\n  return profileDir;\n}\n\nfunction resolve(profilesDir: string, type: string) {\n  return resolveCredentialProfile({\n    type,\n    cwd: profilesDir,\n    env: { OMNIHARNESS_CREDENTIAL_PROFILES_DIR: profilesDir, PATH: process.env.PATH },\n  });\n}\n\ndescribe(\"credential provider failures\", () => {\n  it(\"reports the exit code and the provider's own stderr\", async () => {\n    const profilesDir = createProfilesDir();\n    writeProviderProfile(profilesDir, \"claude\", `#!/usr/bin/env node\nprocess.stderr.write('Runner auth refresh failed (401): {\"error\":\"invalid_refresh_token\"}\\\\n');\nprocess.exit(1);\n`);\n\n    await expect(resolve(profilesDir, \"claude\")).rejects.toThrow(\n      'Credential profile \"claude\" provider command failed (exit 1). Runner auth refresh failed (401): {\"error\":\"invalid_refresh_token\"}',\n    );\n  });\n\n  it(\"redacts secrets the provider prints to stderr\", async () => {\n    const profilesDir = createProfilesDir();\n    writeProviderProfile(profilesDir, \"claude\", `#!/usr/bin/env node\nprocess.stderr.write('rejected ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN=[REDACTED]\\\\n');\nprocess.exit(2);\n`);\n\n    const error = await resolve(profilesDir, \"claude\").catch((caught: Error) => caught);\n\n    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(Error);\n    expect((error as Error).message).toContain(\"(exit 2)\");\n    expect((error as Error).message).toContain(\"ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN=[REDACTED]\");\n    expect((error as Error).message).not.toContain(\"[REDACTED]\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"names a provider command that is not on disk\", async () => {\n    const profilesDir = createProfilesDir();\n    const profileDir = join(profilesDir, \"claude\");\n    mkdirSync(profileDir, { recursive: true });\n    const missing = join(profileDir, \"does-not-exist\");\n    writeFileSync(join(profileDir, \"profile.json\"), JSON.stringify({ command: missing }));\n\n    await expect(resolve(profilesDir, \"claude\")).rejects.toThrow(`command not found: ${missing}`);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"resolveCredentialProfilesDir\", () => {\n  it(\"defaults to the app root when no directory is configured\", () => {\n    expect(resolveCredentialProfilesDir({ OMNIHARNESS_ROOT: \"/srv/omni\" }, \"/anywhere\"))\n      .toBe(\"/srv/omni/.omniharness/credential-profiles\");\n    expect(resolveCredentialProfilesDir({ OMNIHARNESS_ROOT: \"/srv/omni\", OMNIHARNESS_CREDENTIAL_PROFILES_DIR: \"  \" }, \"/anywhere\"))\n      .toBe(\"/srv/omni/.omniharness/credential-profiles\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"resolves a configured directory, relative to the given cwd\", () => {\n    expect(resolveCredentialProfilesDir({ OMNIHARNESS_CREDENTIAL_PROFILES_DIR: \"/etc/omni-profiles\" }, \"/anywhere\"))\n      .toBe(\"/etc/omni-profiles\");\n    expect(resolveCredentialProfilesDir({ OMNIHARNESS_CREDENTIAL_PROFILES_DIR: \"profiles\" }, \"/anywhere\"))\n      .toBe(\"/anywhere/profiles\");\n    expect(resolveCredentialProfilesDir({ OMNIHARNESS_CREDENTIAL_PROFILES_DIR: \"~/profiles\", HOME: \"/home/omni\" }, \"/anywhere\"))\n      .toBe(\"/home/omni/profiles\");\n  });\n});\n"
}
```

## Claude · 2026-09-09T09:36:04.907Z

Both done.

**1. Deleted** `.omniharness/credential-profiles/claude/` (its only content was the `profile.json` pointing at `/Users/masterman/.local/bin/baton credential-profile`; the path was gitignored, so if you ever want it back it's the four-line JSON from earlier in this conversation). The parent `credential-profiles/` directory is now empty. Account-less claude spawns will fall through to the local session bridge instead of Runner.

**2. Fixed both halves of the invisibility bug.**

`external-credentials.ts` — `resolveProfilesDir` is now exported as `resolveCredentialProfilesDir`, the one place that decides where profiles live. The provider failure now carries the exit status and the provider's own stderr:

```
Credential profile "claude" provider command failed (exit 1). Runner auth refresh failed (401): {"error":"invalid_refresh_token"}
```

It distinguishes `command not found: /path`, `timed out after 5000ms`, and signal deaths. stderr is redacted before it goes into the message — `NAME=secret` style assignments, `authorization:` headers, and bare `sk-`/`gh*_`/JWT values — then whitespace-collapsed and capped at 300 chars, since that text lands in the UI banner and in `execution_events`.

`migration.ts:440` — the account importer now resolves the profiles directory through the same helper, so a blank `OMNIHARNESS_CREDENTIAL_PROFILES_DIR` setting means the default `<app root>/.omniharness/credential-profiles`, exactly as the runtime reads it. A profile dropped there becomes a visible `credential-profile-<type>` account you can disable, instead of silently applying.

**Tests:** new `tests/server/agent-runtime/external-credentials.test.ts` (5 tests: exit code + stderr passthrough, secret redaction, missing command, and the directory defaulting rules) plus a migration test that a profile in the default directory is imported with the setting blank. All pass, along with the existing accounts suites and the runtime credential integration tests in `http.test.ts`.

`tsc --noEmit` is clean apart from one pre-existing error in `tests/app/conversation-transcript-retention.test.ts` (a `never` assignment from commit 15d87bd), which is unrelated to these files.

One caveat on the importer fix: the account migration runs in the Next.js process while credentials resolve in the agent-runtime process, so their `process.cwd()` could in principle differ. Setting `OMNIHARNESS_ROOT` makes both exact; without it they agree as long as both start from the repo root.
